TL;DR: 本文提出RoomWright，一个以功能使用为导向的智能体框架，用于生成完全以代码表示的3D场景，支持具身交互。该框架通过使用驱动的物体推理、多部分交互编译（触发-条件-效果规则）以及注释引导的方向恢复机制，生成可执行、可编辑、可模拟的交互式场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于代码的场景生成方法虽然能生成可编辑和可扩展的环境，但仅关注视觉构建和物体级别的铰接，未能建模场景的功能性使用方式，导致生成的场景无法支持具身交互和策略学习。

Method: RoomWright采用智能体驱动的使用导向框架：1) 使用驱动的物体推理，将每个锚点视为任务中心并纳入任务所需物体及其可操作性；2) 代码智能体通过将交互编译为触发-条件-效果规则，更新结构化物体状态，捕获物体间的因果依赖；3) 通过注释信息辅助的使用导向方向恢复方法，解决可操作物体方向模糊且难以从像素恢复的问题。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法有效，生成的场景具备可执行、可编辑和可模拟的特性，为具身AI和策略学习提供了交互式环境。

Conclusion: RoomWright成功弥补了现有场景生成方法在功能性使用建模方面的不足，为具身AI提供了支持交互的代码化场景表示，推动了策略学习和机器人操控等领域的发展。

Abstract: Indoor scene synthesis provides essential environments for embodied AI, robotic manipulation, and simulation-based policy learning. Recent code-based scene generation methods produce editable and extensible environments, yet they remain focused on visual construction and object-level articulation, leaving the functional usage of scenes largely unmodeled. To address this problem, we present RoomWright, an agentic usage-driven framework for generating 3D scenes represented entirely as code for embodied interaction. RoomWright performs usage-driven object reasoning, which treats each anchor as a task centre and admits task-required objects and their affordances. A code agent further enables multi-part interaction by compiling each interaction into a trigger, condition, effect rule that updates structured object states, capturing causal dependencies across objects. Moreover, since manipuland orientation is ambiguous and hard to recover from pixels, RoomWright alleviates this via annotation-informed usage-guided orientation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. The resulting scenes are executable, editable, and simulation-ready, providing interactive environments for embodied AI and policy learning.

</details>


### [173] [RoboEdit: Turning Human Manipulation Videos into Scalable Robot Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18948)
*Yaowei Guo,Zeng Tao,Yuxin Jiang,Yunuo Chen,Zhiyang Dou,Yuxiang Ma,Yin Yang,Demetri Terzopoulos,Ying Jiang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboEdit，一个将人类操作视频转换为动作一致、物理合理的机器人视频的编辑套件，包含自动数据管道（RoboEdit-ADC）、大规模数据集（RoboEdit-14M，含174K个对齐视频对，共1400万帧，覆盖7种机器人形态）以及核心编辑引擎（RoboEdit-Trans），并集成了3D机器人状态解码器以恢复逐帧手部状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收集机器人手-物交互数据成本高昂且受限于具体形态，而大量人类操作视频无法直接用于机器人训练，亟需一种方法将丰富的人类视频转化为可用的机器人训练数据。

Method: RoboEdit包含三个核心组件：(1) RoboEdit-ADC自动管道，从RGB视频中重建并重定向3D交互到不同机器人形态；(2) RoboEdit-Trans核心编辑引擎，采用跨形态适应模块在保持时间连贯性的同时调整外观和运动，并集成3D机器人状态解码器恢复逐帧手部状态用于结构化运动监督；(3) RoboEdit-14M大规模数据集，由上述管道自动生成。

Result: 实验表明RoboEdit在编辑质量上达到最先进水平，并能在真实世界操作任务中支持下游机器人控制策略，验证了其生成数据的有效性。

Conclusion: RoboEdit套件充分释放了无标注人类视频的潜力，为通用机器人学习提供了可扩展、高保真的视觉和3D运动监督信号，有助于解决机器人数据稀缺问题。

Abstract: Collecting robot hand-object interaction data is costly and embodiment-specific, yet abundant human-object videos remain unusable for robot training. We present RoboEdit, a human-to-robot video editing suite that transforms human manipulation videos into action-consistent, physically plausible robot videos with aligned 3D hand states. To enable scalable supervision, we introduce RoboEdit-ADC, an automatic pipeline that reconstructs and retargets 3D interactions from RGB videos across embodiments. This pipeline generates RoboEdit-14M, a large-scale dataset of 174K aligned video pairs (14M frames) spanning seven robot embodiments, diverse scenes, and interaction types. The core editing engine, RoboEdit-Trans, employs cross-embodiment adaptation modules to preserve temporal coherence while adapting appearance and motion. It further integrates a 3D Robot-State Decoder to recover per-frame hand states for structured motion supervision. Experiments show that RoboEdit achieves state-of-the-art editing quality and supports downstream robot control policies in real-world manipulation tasks. Ultimately, the RoboEdit suite unlocks the vast potential of unlabeled human videos, providing scalable, high-fidelity visual and 3D motion supervision for generalizable robot learning.

</details>


### [174] [Autonomous Agricultural Tractor: Integrated Weed Detection and LiDAR Navigation for Precision Paddy Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19004)
*Benjamin Merryman-Smith,Tony Nguyen,Bilal Dogutas,Krish Shah,Anthony Raphael,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AgriNav，一个集成式自主拖拉机系统，通过四个ROS耦合模块（基于PyTorch的WeedDet水稻检测、轻量级CNN-FPN变体、硬编码置信门控保护机制、六状态扩展卡尔曼滤波器）解决稻田定点杂草管理的三大挑战，核心创新是四机制LiDAR-相机融合桥，在零额外硬件成本下实现区域约束、世界坐标投影、地面滤波和双向置信融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稻田定点杂草管理相比传统广播喷洒可大幅减少除草剂使用，但实际部署面临三大障碍：冠层下GNSS信号退化导致的行导航鲁棒性问题、水稻与形态多样杂草的实时视觉区分难题、以及误将水稻识别为杂草的不可逆不对称成本。

Method: 系统由四个ROS耦合模块组成：（1）自定义PyTorch实现的WeedDet用于水稻检测；（2）1.68M参数的轻量级CNN-FPN变体，采用不对称类别加权；（3）基于硬编码置信门控否决的反向逻辑判别模块，保护水稻类别；（4）融合GNSS、IMU和轮式里程计并具备三级中断桥接的六状态恒定速度转弯率扩展卡尔曼滤波器。核心贡献是四机制LiDAR-相机融合桥，利用导航LiDAR实现感兴趣区域约束、世界坐标投影、地面平面滤波和双向置信融合。

Result: 仿真实验表明：系统在20秒GNSS中断期间保持连续位置跟踪，作物行检测置信度始终高于0.9，水稻检测置信度在稻田、航拍和洪水后图像上达到0.32至0.95，LiDAR ROI约束使检测推理区域减少约30%至50%。

Conclusion: AgriNav通过多传感器融合架构有效解决了稻田自主杂草管理的导航、检测和误分类保护三大难题，且融合策略无需额外硬件成本，为定点杂草管理的实际田间部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Site-specific weed management in paddy farming offers substantial reductions in herbicide use over conventional broadcast spraying, but field deployment has been limited by three persistent challenges: robust crop-row navigation under canopy where GNSS degrades, real-time visual discrimination between rice and morphologically diverse weeds, and the asymmetric cost of misclassifying rice as weed, which is irreversible.
  This paper presents AgriNav, an integrated autonomous tractor system built around four ROS-coupled modules: a custom PyTorch reimplementation of WeedDet for rice detection, a parallel lightweight 1.68M-parameter CNN-FPN variant with asymmetric class weighting, an inverted-logic discrimination module that protects the rice class through a hardcoded confidence-gate veto, and a 6-state constant-velocity-turn-rate Extended Kalman Filter fusing GNSS, IMU, and wheel odometry with three-level outage bridging. Our primary system-level contribution is a four-mechanism LiDAR-camera fusion bridge that uses the navigation LiDAR for region-of-interest constraint, world-coordinate projection, ground-plane filtering, and bidirectional confidence fusion at zero additional hardware cost.
  Simulation experiments demonstrate continuous position tracking through a 20-second GNSS outage, crop row detection confidence above 0.9 throughout operation, and rice-detection confidences from 0.32 to 0.95 across paddy, aerial, and post-flood imagery. The LiDAR ROI constraint reduces detection inference region by an estimated 30 to 50 percent.

</details>


### [175] [LT-Mem: Volatility-Aware Spatio-Temporal Memory for Lifelong Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19059)
*Yumin Lee,Hyoseok Ju,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LT-Mem 提出了一种波动性感知的记忆演化框架，用于长时间运行的机器人在多变环境中保留跨会话的对象级记忆，并通过 Tri-Memory 结构（Live、Delta、Meta）实现纵向对象推理，同时在 LT-VQA 数据集上以极少 token 消耗超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统要么覆盖历史以维护最新地图，要么存储语义快照却缺乏跨会话对象身份一致性，导致"时间失忆"——无法回答诸如"绿色椅子在所有会话中出现在哪里"这类需要对象历史追踪的问题。

Method: LT-Mem 包含三部分：(1) 多会话SLAM骨干提供空间对齐的逐对象观测；(2) 推理层通过确定性证据评分保持跨会话身份，并采用波动性感知策略在覆盖、保持、多假设三种动作间选择；(3) Tri-Memory结构（Live、Delta、Meta）同时保存当前状态和事件历史。同时引入LT-VQA数据集，包含多会话记录、持久身份标注和时间问答对。

Result: 实验表明LT-Mem在所有指标上持续优于基线，同时消耗的token数量少一个数量级。消融实验证实性能提升来自结构化记忆架构而非LLM容量。

Conclusion: LT-Mem通过波动性感知的结构化记忆演化机制，有效解决了长时间机器人操作中的对象级时间失忆问题，为面向长期自主运行的机器人场景理解提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Long-term robot operation in evolving environments requires object-level understanding that persists across repeated revisits. Existing systems either overwrite history to maintain an up-to-date map or store semantic snapshots without consistent cross-session object identity, resulting in temporal amnesia: the systematic loss of object history that prevents answering queries such as "Where has the green chair been across all sessions?" We propose LT-Mem, a volatility-aware memory evolution framework that unifies spatially aligned instance-level 3D perception with volatility-conditioned temporal reasoning. First, a multi-session SLAM backbone provides spatially aligned per-object observations across sessions. Second, a reasoning layer governs how object memory evolves: deterministic evidence scoring preserves cross-session identity, and a volatility-aware policy selects among overwrite, hold, and multi-hypothesis actions based on each object's dynamics. Third, the resulting Tri-Memory structure (Live, Delta, Meta) preserves both current states and event histories, enabling longitudinal object-centric reasoning. We further introduce LT-VQA, a dataset and evaluation suite comprising multi-session recordings, persistent identity annotations, and temporal QA pairs. Experiments show that LT-Mem consistently outperforms baselines across all metrics while consuming an order of magnitude fewer tokens, and ablations confirm that gains are driven by the structured memory architecture rather than LLM capacity.

</details>


### [176] [DA-WAM: Decision-Aligned Future Latents for Driving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19085)
*Ruiguo Zhong,Benshan Ma,Xiaolong Chen,Lang Zhang,Mingyue Feng,Yaonong Wang,Pei Liu,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DA-WAM框架，将预测性表征学习、动作条件未来建模和轨迹评分统一到单一决策目标下，通过在线编码器和动量目标维持预测监督，并利用动作条件预测器为每个轨迹候选生成独特未来潜在状态，最终在NAVSIM基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型将未来表征学习与规划优化解耦，或在不同轨迹候选间共享预测状态，导致预测的未来无法直接指导轨迹选择，缺乏决策信息性（decision-informative）的未来建模。

Method: DA-WAM包含三个关键组件：(1) 在线编码器与稳定动量目标共同维持规划优化过程中的预测监督，使未来表征与驾驶任务协同演化；(2) 动作条件预测器为每条轨迹候选生成独特的未来潜在状态，保留动作特定的后果差异；(3) 未来潜在条件因子化评分器对候选未来进行评估，同时引入专家匹配轨迹的观测未来监督和安全关键硬负样本增强规划边界处的监督信号。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v1和NAVSIM-v2基准上实现了最先进的性能，消融实验和诊断分析验证了各关键组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将预测性表征学习、动作条件未来建模和轨迹评分在单一决策目标下统一，DA-WAM实现了未来建模真正服务于规划决策的目的，为自动驾驶世界模型的应用提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Anticipating how scenes evolve under ego actions is fundamental to safe autonomous driving, yet the full potential of world models for decision-making remains unrealized. The critical challenge lies in ensuring that future modeling is not merely predictive, but decision-informative: the predicted future must directly shape which trajectory is selected. Existing approaches decouple future representation learning from planning optimization, or share predicted states across trajectory candidates, thereby diluting the action-specific consequences that ought to guide selection. To bridge this gap, we propose DA-WAM, a framework that unifies predictive representation learning, action-conditioned future modeling, and trajectory scoring under a single decision-making objective. DA-WAM maintains predictive supervision throughout planner optimization via an online encoder and a stable momentum target, allowing future representations to co-evolve with the driving task. An action-conditioned predictor generates a distinct future latent state per trajectory candidate, which is then evaluated by a future-latent-conditioned factorized scorer. For the expert-matched trajectory, the predicted future latent is supervised by the observed future representation, while safety-critical hard negatives provide additional supervision near planning boundaries. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM-v1 and NAVSIM-v2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, while ablations and diagnostic analyses validate the key components.

</details>


### [177] [ADEPT: Accelerating Dexterity via Pre-Training and Post-Training using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19182)
*Jayjun Lee,Jessica Yin,Asif Rana,Nicholas Blauch,Sam Mady,Mohak Bhardwaj,Nima Fazeli,Nathan Ratliff,Karl Van Wyk,Ankur Handa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ADEPT框架，通过大规模强化学习预训练，实现高自由度机器人在原始视觉-触觉感知下完成长时程灵巧操作任务的模拟到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指机器人的灵巧操作任务难以从零开始学习，且不同下游任务需要重复学习相同的技能，缺乏可迁移的灵巧行为预训练方案。

Method: ADEPT通过在通用物体重新摆放任务上预训练灵巧策略作为先验，采用行为克隆蒸馏、评论家预热和保守on-policy更新的稳定后训练方案，并引入关节空间Geometric Fabric中介RL策略与机器人，最终将后训练的教师模型蒸馏为感知学生模型。

Result: 在23自由度Kuka-Allegro（双RGB相机）和29自由度Flexiv-Sharpa（双RGB相机加五个视觉触觉传感器）两种平台上实现了零样本模拟到现实迁移，能以人类级速度解决长时程任务并进行初始状态挑战。

Conclusion: ADEPT证明大规模预训练灵巧策略可有效加速新行为的学习并实现跨实体sim-to-real迁移，且需结合稳定的后训练策略以避免预训练能力退化。

Abstract: We introduce Accelerating Dexterity via Pre-Training (ADEPT), a large-scale reinforcement learning (RL) framework for learning sim-to-real transferable dexterity across high degree-of-freedom (DoF) robot embodiments that can solve long-horizon tasks directly from raw visuo-tactile perception. ADEPT pretrains a dexterous policy on a generic object reposing task, then post-trains downstream policies with this pretrained behavior as a prior. ADEPT enables learning new behaviors that are otherwise difficult to discover from scratch on multi-fingered robots and avoids learning the same set of skills over again for every new downstream task. The pretrained policy zero-shots the reposing phase of downstream tasks, but naïve RL fine-tuning rapidly degrades this capability during transfer. We address this with a stable post-training recipe combining behavior-cloning distillation, critic warm-up, and conservative on-policy updates. To safely exploit the full kinematic dexterity, we introduce a joint-space Geometric Fabric that mediates between the RL policy and the robot. We distill post-trained teachers into perceptive students that zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on two embodiments: a 23 DoF Kuka-Allegro with two RGB cameras, and a 29 DoF Flexiv-Sharpa with two RGB cameras and five vision-based tactile sensors, and can solve long-horizon tasks from challenging initial states with dexterity at human-level speed.

</details>


### [178] [PartialBiGrasp: Inferring Hidden Local Geometry for Bimanual Grasping from Partial Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19188)
*Ayush Kaura,Vignesh Vembar,Md Faizal Karim,Keshab Patra,K Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PartialBiGrasp，一种基于部分点云观测的双臂抓取生成框架，通过卷积占用网络隐式学习几何特征，生成力闭合抓取对，并利用基于采样的优化修正因几何不完整导致的歧义，在仿真和真实机器人上验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双臂抓取方法依赖完整点云信息，但在实际场景中往往只能获得部分点云观测，导致这些方法难以适用。大型复杂物体的可抓取区域稀疏且依赖局部几何属性，因此需要一种能够在部分观测条件下工作的双臂抓取框架。

Method: 提出PartialBiGrasp框架，使用卷积占用网络（Convolutional Occupancy Networks）隐式学习几何特征，进行关于可抓取性、无碰撞接触区域和物体厚度的局部推理，生成力闭合（force-closure）抓取对，并通过基于采样的优化进行细化，以校正不完整几何信息带来的歧义。

Result: 通过解析力闭合指标、大规模仿真实验以及真实机器人上对含有噪声的部分点云新物体的评估，验证了该方法能够生成稳健且物理稳定的双臂抓取。

Conclusion: PartialBiGrasp能够在部分点云观测下实现可靠的双臂抓取，填补了实际场景中点云不完整条件的应用空白，为大型复杂物体的双臂操作提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Dual-arm robotic grasping is essential for manipulating large, heavy, and geometrically complex objects that cannot be reliably handled using a single manipulator. These large objects often contain only sparse graspable regions determined by local geometric properties such as thickness, edge structure, and gripper clearance. Prior bimanual grasping methods assume access to a full point cloud of the object which inherently contains this geometric information, but may not be accessible in real scenarios. This work proposes PartialBiGrasp, a dual-arm grasp generation framework that operates directly on partial point cloud observations. Our model learns geometric features implicitly through convolutional occupancy networks, enabling local reasoning about graspability, collision-free contact regions, and object thickness. We leverage this understanding to generate force-closure compliant grasp pairs, which are further refined using a sampling-based optimization to correct for ambiguity caused by incomplete geometry. We evaluate our approach using analytical force-closure metrics, large-scale simulation experiments, and real-world robot evaluations on noisy partial point clouds of novel objects, demonstrating robust and physically stable dual-arm grasp generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Entropy-Constrained Adaptive Stochastic Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18147)
*Ran Ben Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Michael Mitzenmacher,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出熵约束自适应随机量化（ECASQ）问题，在MSE最小化目标下同时考虑熵预算和无偏性约束，并给出最优动态规划算法及GPU友好的近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无偏量化方法（包括ASQ）在选择量化值时未考虑后续的熵编码阶段，导致精度损失。作者希望联合优化量化值选择与熵编码，以进一步提升压缩性能。

Method: 将问题形式化为熵约束下的自适应随机量化（ECASQ），设计O(sd^2)时间和O(d^2)空间的最优动态规划算法，以及O(sd^2)时间和O(d)空间的GPU友好近似动态规划算法。近似算法保证解不劣于使用少一比特熵的最优解，并引入迭代细化程序提升近似解质量。

Result: 实验表明迭代细化过程可获得接近最优的结果，同时相比最优解求解器保持显著的加速优势。

Conclusion: ECASQ通过联合优化量化与熵编码缓解了通信和内存瓶颈，在保持无偏性的同时更有效地利用熵预算，为模型、梯度和KV缓存压缩等场景提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Adaptive stochastic quantization (ASQ) is a recently introduced quantization approach that optimizes the Mean Squared Error (MSE) for a given input while preserving unbiasedness. It is designed to alleviate the communication and memory bottlenecks of modern data and machine learning workloads, including model, gradient, and KV-cache compression and nearest-neighbor search. Further, practical systems can then compress quantized data with a lossless entropy encoder. However, existing unbiased methods, including ASQ, choose their quantization values without considering this later encoding stage, leaving accuracy on the table.
  We formulate the Entropy Constrained Adaptive Stochastic Quantization (ECASQ) problem, which jointly selects adaptive quantization values to minimize MSE under an entropy budget and an unbiasedness constraint. We give an optimal dynamic program with $O(sd^2)$ time and $O(d^2)$ space for a length-d vector and at most s quantization values, and a GPU-friendly approximate dynamic program with $O(sd^2)$ time and $O(d)$ space. The approximation guarantees that the solution has an MSE no larger than the optimal solution that uses one fewer bit of entropy per entry. We also provide an iterative refinement procedure for the approximation solution that, in our experiments, yields near-optimal results while retaining a substantial speed advantage over our solver for the optimal solution.

</details>


### [180] [Towards Reversible Forgetting: Managing Obsolete Knowledge in Continual Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18177)
*Nilutpaul Sarker Yash,Tirtho Roy,Ushashi Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可逆遗忘（reversible forgetting）概念框架，用于企业AI代理在非平稳环境下管理知识状态。通过主动/休眠/退休三种记忆状态及滞回控制器，在减少过时知识负面影响的同时保留恢复能力，避免永久擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习将遗忘视为失败，一味追求知识保留。但在企业AI代理面对的非平稳环境中（客户、政策、工具、工作流、法规、市场条件变化），无差别保留过时知识会引发负迁移和运营风险。因此需要一种既能减少过时信息影响、又不混淆临时抑制与永久删除的遗忘机制。

Method: 提出三种可操作记忆状态（active/dormant/retired）及激活转换机制。实例化为滞回可逆记忆控制器（Hysteretic Reversible Memory Controller）：累积相关性证据、使用非对称阈值防止状态振荡、在影子模式测试再激活、通过策略门控退休流程。

Result: 该框架能够减少过时信息对决策的影响，同时不将临时抑制与永久擦除混为一谈。以金融领域为例说明：一种市场制度下有用的知识在另一制度下可能有害，但当相似条件重现时可重新恢复相关性。

Conclusion: 可逆遗忘为企业AI代理提供了比传统持续学习更精细的知识管理方案，在遗忘与保留之间取得平衡，兼顾环境适应性与历史知识复用能力。

Abstract: Continual learning has traditionally treated forgetting as a failure, emphasizing preservation of previously acquired knowledge as environments evolve. We argue that this objective is incomplete for enterprise AI agents operating in non-stationary environments, where customers, policies, tools, workflows, regulations, and market conditions change over time. Indiscriminate retention can allow obsolete knowledge to influence decisions, creating negative transfer and operational risk. We therefore propose reversible forgetting: a conceptual framework with three operational memory states: active, dormant, and retired, and a reactivation transition that can restore dormant knowledge when its relevance returns. We instantiate the framework as a Hysteretic Reversible Memory Controller that accumulates relevance evidence, uses asymmetric thresholds to prevent state oscillation, tests reactivation in shadow mode, and gates retirement through policy. The framework reduces the influence of obsolete information without conflating temporary suppression with permanent erasure. Finance illustrates the idea: knowledge useful under one market regime may become harmful under another yet regain relevance when similar conditions recur.

</details>


### [181] [Accelerating Visual On-Policy Distillation with Batched Speculative Jacobi Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18183)
*Bingqi Shan,Zhehao Yu,Kenhong Lin,Baoquan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HB-SJD，一种批量化的投机雅可比解码（SJD）回滚后端，用于视觉在线策略蒸馏（OPD），在无需辅助草稿模型的情况下并行处理多个token，同时根据每张图像的独立解码进度动态切换Full与Compact执行模式，显著降低训练时间并保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉OPD训练中，在线回放使用自回归解码逐token生成，每步训练成本高昂。虽然SJD可并行处理多token且无需草稿模型，但原始方法仅支持单序列推理，无法直接用于批量训练场景。

Method: HB-SJD作为批量SJD回滚后端，允许每张图像根据自身解码进度独立推进，同时将不同序列位置的图像在批量模型前向中统一验证。当图像完成解码时，HB-SJD在Full和Compact执行模式间切换，以降低后续回滚轮次的成本。该方法仅替换学生回滚后端，不改变教师模型、蒸馏目标和优化流程。

Result: 在LlamaGen上的实验表明，HB-SJD大幅减少了回滚时间和端到端训练时间，同时保持了蒸馏学生的生成质量。

Conclusion: HB-SJD有效解决了视觉OPD中SJD无法批量化的瓶颈，在降低训练成本的同时不牺牲模型质量，为紧凑视觉自回归模型的高效蒸馏提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Visual on-policy distillation (OPD) improves the training of compact visual autoregressive models by learning from trajectories generated by the current student. However, these online rollouts are still produced token by token with autoregressive decoding, which adds substantial cost to every on-policy training step. Speculative Jacobi Decoding (SJD) provides an alternative because it can process multiple tokens in parallel without an auxiliary draft model, but the original method is designed for single-sequence inference. We introduce HB-SJD, a batched SJD rollout backend for visual OPD. HB-SJD allows each image to advance independently according to its own decoding progress, while images at different sequence positions are still verified in batched model forwards. As images finish, HB-SJD switches between Full and Compact execution to reduce the cost of later rollout rounds. HB-SJD only replaces the student rollout backend and leaves the teacher, distillation objective, and optimization procedure unchanged. Experiments with LlamaGen show that HB-SJD substantially reduces rollout and end-to-end training time while preserving the generation quality of the distilled student.

</details>


### [182] [H$^2$EDL: Hyper Evidential Deep Learning for Hierarchical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18185)
*Yuanye Liu,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出H²EDL模型，通过将分类树结构作为超域，在每个分支节点上定义局部Dirichlet意见，从而在细粒度层级分类中实现同时捕获叶子类别和中间概念的树结构化超意见不确定性表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度识别涉及层级标签空间，现有方法存在不足：扁平证据分类器只能给出单一空泛（vacuity）值，无法捕捉中间概念的不确定性；层级分类器只传播点概率，缺乏证据概念。已有的超证据网络要么需要训练数据提供复合标签，要么从非结构化权重中读取信息，缺乏原则性的复合标签选择依据。

Method: 观察到分类树本身即缺失的超域：其子树和叶子单例构成线性大小的焦族。在每个分支节点上定义一个局部Dirichlet意见，使得所有复合质量可以闭式导出。H²EDL同时提供两种互补解释：作为预测工具时是保持层级间一致性的层级分类器；作为概率工具时定义了有效的树结构化超意见，节点上的质量代表到达该节点但不足以进一步细分的信念。

Result: 在FGVC-Aircraft和DERM12345数据集上，H²EDL相比交叉熵基线将校准误差降低约一半，该改进在更深层级和更大训练预算下更为显著。

Conclusion: H²EDL通过利用分类树本身的结构来构建超意见，成功统一了证据分类器与层级分类器的优势，为细粒度层级识别中的结构化不确定性建模提供了有效且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-grained recognition often involves hierarchical label spaces, where a model may be confident about a coarse semantic concept while remaining uncertain among its descendant classes. Such structured ambiguity requires uncertainty representations that capture both fine-grained classes and intermediate concepts. However, existing tools each capture only half of it: flat evidential classifiers quantify total ignorance with a single vacuity on the leaf frame, and hierarchical classifiers propagate point probabilities with no notion of evidence. Hyper-opinions would unify the two, but their general form is exponential in the label count, and existing hyper-evidential networks either require composite labels to be supplied in the training data or read them off an unstructured weight pattern, with no principled notion of which composites deserve mass. We observe that the taxonomy itself is the missing hyperdomain. Its subtrees and leaf singletons form a linear-size focal family, and one local Dirichlet opinion per branching node induces every composite mass in closed form. The resulting model, H$^2$EDL, can be interpreted in two complementary ways using the same set of parameters. From a prediction perspective, it functions as a hierarchical classifier that preserves consistency across different levels of the label tree. From a probabilistic perspective, it defines a valid tree-structured hyper-opinion, where the mass assigned to each node represents the belief that reaches that node but does not provide sufficient confidence to further specialize into its descendants. On FGVC-Aircraft and DERM12345, H$^2$EDL reduces calibration error by approximately half compared with cross-entropy baselines, with the improvement becoming more pronounced at deeper hierarchy levels and under larger training budgets.

</details>


### [183] [What Can Artificial Intelligence Learn from Medicine? Generative Analogies and Reliable Machine Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18186)
*Emanuele Ratti,Lena Zuchowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Hesse的类比理论，将机器学习（ML）在医学中的认知与方法论辩护，与临床转化的标准进行生成性类比，提出一种新型的ML可靠主义认识论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习在医学中已广泛应用，但其认知与方法论依据仍不明确。文献中虽已有人将医学与ML进行类比，但缺乏对这一类比本质的深入刻画。

Method: 运用Hesse的工作工具，将临床转化过程与ML系统构建过程之间的平行关系刻画为一种生成性类比，并从可靠主义角度解读临床转化的认知与方法论辩护，进而将其类比性地应用于ML语境。

Result: 揭示了临床转化中通常仅被提及而未细化的认知与方法论辩护，并展示了这些辩护如何在类比意义上适用于ML，最终提出一种区别于现有哲学AI可靠主义（但与之兼容）的新形式ML可靠主义。

Conclusion: 通过生成性类比，可以为ML在医学中的认知与方法论地位提供更坚实的哲学基础，形成一种新的、与既有框架兼容的ML可靠主义认识论。

Abstract: In the past few years, machine learning (ML) has been widely (and to an extent, successfully) implemented in medicine. However, uncertainties surrounding ML have made it difficult to establish the bases of its epistemic and methodological warrants. In the literature, a parallel has been drawn between medicine and ML, suggesting that we should model epistemic and methodological standards for ML on the standards of clinical translation. By developing tools from Hesse work, we characterise the nature of this parallel as a generative analogy between the process of clinical translation and the process of building ML systems. We identify more precisely the epistemic and methodological warrants of clinical translation that are typically only mentioned when appealing to the analogy, and we show in which sense such warrants apply analogically to the context of ML. In particular, we interpret warrants of clinical translation in reliabilist terms, and we show how this can inform a new form of ML reliabilism, which is distinct from (though compatible with) existing reliabilist accounts in philosophy of AI.

</details>


### [184] [A systematic review of machine learning techniques to address diagnosis and treatment of autism: challenges and opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18188)
*Rafael Muñoz-Terol,Jesús Peral,Sandra Amador,David Gil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾了2017至2023年间55项关于机器学习在自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）中应用的研究，总结了ML技术在ASD诊断与治疗中的趋势、技术与数据集使用情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD的病因尚不明确，识别相关特征和隐藏相关性对于早期诊断至关重要，因此需要系统梳理近年ML在ASD研究中的应用现状。

Method: 对2017至2023年间发表的55项研究进行系统性回顾，分析监督学习、深度学习及混合方法在ASD诊断和治疗中的应用趋势。

Result: 监督学习方法占据主导地位，与ASD诊断需求高度契合；深度学习由于数据可用性增加而应用不断扩展；未来混合方法（结合无监督、深度学习和模糊逻辑）值得关注。

Conclusion: 解决当前挑战需要跨学科合作和针对ASD扩展的数据集；未来的ML模型应从多模态数据整合中受益，以更全面地应对ASD的复杂性。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) is a developmental disability characterized by challenges in social interaction and communication. As the causes of ASD remain unclear, identifying relevant features and hidden correlations is crucial for early diagnosis. This systematic review evaluates 55 studies from 2017 to 2023 on the application of machine learning (ML) techniques to ASD. The primary objective is to examine recent ML applications in ASD research, identifying trends, techniques, and datasets that enhance diagnosis and treatment. Supervised learning methods dominate, as they align well with ASD diagnostic needs; however, the role of deep learning is expanding with greater data availability. Emerging techniques based on hybrid methods, where unsupervised, deep learning, and fuzzy logic could be included, will be interesting to observe in the future. The review highlights key challenges and opportunities, particularly the need for models that can integrate complex data -such as genetic and clinical information- to improve diagnostic accuracy and treatment outcomes. Additionally, incorporating innovative data sources, like wearable devices and biometric sensors, could enable continuous and non-intrusive monitoring, providing a more holistic understanding of ASD. Findings emphasize that addressing current challenges requires interdisciplinary collaboration and expanded datasets tailored to ASD. Future ML models will benefit from broader multimodal data integration, enabling researchers to more comprehensively address the complexities of ASD.

</details>


### [185] [Safe Domain Adaptation for Physics: Overcoming Nuisances, Label Shifts, and Simulation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18190)
*Ivan Kharuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了域适应在物理领域中的应用问题，指出传统假设（两域仅在干扰项上不同）在物理中不成立，因为模拟可能错且目标物理量的分布本身就是要测量的对象。作者提出自适应域适应方法，通过对模拟事件重新加权使域适应集中于真实的物理失配，并提供无标签模型选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统域适应假设模拟域与实验域仅在噪声/干扰上不同，且目标物理量在两域中分布相同。但在物理应用中，这两个假设都不成立：模拟可能对物理本身有误，而能量谱、红移分布等目标量往往正是需要测量的对象。这种失配会导致标准对抗式域适应将两个不同的谱对齐，从而用一个锚定在模拟先验上的有偏结果替换无控制偏差。

Method: 在玩具气簇射基准上分别或联合引入三类失配：探测器响应干扰、物理模拟偏移和能量谱偏移。标准对抗式域适应处理条件偏移，但面对谱差异时会产生对齐问题。作者提出自适应域适应（adaptive domain adaptation），通过对模拟事件重新加权，使域适应专注于真实的物理失配。同时，由于预测谱依赖模型训练配置，提供一种无标签模型选择规则来选择接近最优的工作点。

Result: 标准对抗式域适应能够处理条件偏移（如探测器响应干扰和物理模拟偏移），但当两域能量谱不同时会错误地对齐谱分布，引入依赖模拟先验的偏差。所提出的自适应域适应方法通过重新加权聚焦于真实物理失配，缓解了这一问题，且提出的无标签模型选择规则能在不依赖标签的情况下选出接近最优的工作点。

Conclusion: 物理场景中的域适应需要超越传统假设，因为目标物理量的分布本身就是测量对象。自适应域适应通过事件重加权来区分物理失配与谱差异，结合无标签模型选择规则，为物理领域的域适应提供更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Domain adaptation is widely used to make neural networks trained on simulations applicable to experimental data. Its premise is that the two domains differ only in nuisances, and that the quantity of interest is distributed identically in both. In physics neither assumption holds: simulations can be wrong about the physics, and the distribution of the target quantity - an energy spectrum, a redshift distribution - is often the measurement itself. We study the consequences of such mismatches on a toy air-shower benchmark in which a detector-response nuisance, a physical simulation shift, and an energy-spectrum shift can be switched on separately or together. Standard adversarial adaptation handles the conditional shifts, but once the two spectra differ it aligns them, replacing an uncontrolled bias by one anchored on the simulation prior. We present adaptive domain adaptation, which reweights the simulated events so as to focus domain adaptation on the genuine physical mismatch alone. Since the predicted spectrum depends on model training configuration, we provide a label-free model selection rule for selecting the near-the-best operation point.

</details>


### [186] [ChiroEcho: extending automated bat vocalisation classification beyond the learned taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18191)
*Burooj Ghani,Welmoed Eversteijn,Milan van Hirtum,Juan Sebastián Cañas,Vincent J. Kalkman,Dan Stowell,A. Leonie Baier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个深度学习框架，通过联合预测蝙蝠的物种和属，并在推理时结合属预测与地理分布信息，将地理信息作为扩展分类器有效分类体系的手段而非约束。系统覆盖从35种扩展到41种欧洲蝙蝠（覆盖率从73%提升至85%），是目前覆盖范围最广的自动化欧洲蝙蝠分类系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蝙蝠是欧洲受保护的生态系统健康指标物种，可靠的种群监测至关重要。然而蝙蝠的隐秘夜行性使得声学被动监测成为必要手段，而回声定位叫声随行为和环境的变异以及物种间的重叠使得自动识别困难重重。同时，传统分类器受限于固定的分类体系，无法识别训练数据中未包含的物种。

Method: 构建一个深度学习框架，同时预测蝙蝠的物种和属两个层级。在推理阶段，将属的预测结果与地理物种分布信息相结合：当预测属在一地区仅有一个物种时，即可解析出该物种，即使用户物种分类体系中没有该物种。该方法将地理信息重新定义为扩展分类器有效分类体系的方式。

Result: 基于涵盖35种欧洲蝙蝠物种的录音数据进行评估，包括闭集分类、稀有物种性能估计的不稳定性分析以及受控的留出法概念验证实验。结果显示框架将操作覆盖范围从35种扩展到48种欧洲原生蝙蝠中的41种（覆盖率从73%提升至85%），据作者所知这是目前自动化欧洲蝙蝠分类报告中最广的操作覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 该蝙蝠分类框架为如何通过粗粒度预测与透明外部约束相结合来解析未见过的细粒度类别提供了概念验证，证明了地理信息可以作为一种扩展分类器能力的机制，而非仅仅是约束条件。

Abstract: Bats are key indicators of ecosystem health and are protected throughout Europe, making reliable population monitoring a conservation priority. Their cryptic nocturnal lifestyle makes passive acoustic monitoring essential, yet automated identification remains difficult as echolocation calls vary with behaviour and environment and overlap among species. We present a deep learning framework that jointly predicts species and genus and combines genus predictions with geographic species distributions at inference. When only one species of a predicted genus occurs in a region, the framework can resolve species absent from the learned taxonomy. This reframes geographic information as a means of extending, rather than constraining, a classifier's effective taxonomy. Using recordings spanning 35 European bat species, we evaluate closed-set classification, examine the instability of performance estimates for sparsely represented species, and conduct a controlled held-out proof-of-principle experiment. The rare-species analysis shows how limited evaluation data can obscure species-level performance, while the held-out experiment shows that genus predictions and location can recover labels unavailable to the species head. Geographic resolution extends operational coverage from 35 to 41 of the 48 native European bat species, increasing coverage from 73% to 85%. To our knowledge, this is the broadest operational coverage reported for automated European bat classification. More broadly, the bat framework provides proof of principle for resolving unseen fine-grained classes by combining coarse predictions with transparent external constraints.

</details>


### [187] [Think Shallow, Solve Deep: Controlling Recurrent Dynamics for Reliable Test-Time Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18222)
*Ivan Viakhirev,Kirill Borodin,Amirah Almutairi,Serguei Barannikov,Maxim Abramov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究递归深度推理器在测试时增加迭代次数对答案的影响，发现训练操作符的有限时间动力学机制（settling/marginal/drifting）可预测深度行为，并提出深度安全性的充分条件：当每步位移小于解码器边际时，答案不再变化。实验表明settling操作符不会因增加深度而退化，在Sudoku等任务上甚至可将准确率从0.19提升到0.34。单一终端不动点目标可同时改变动力学机制与深度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归深度推理器旨在通过测试时更多迭代解决更难问题，但额外迭代可能提升、保持或降低答案质量，缺乏对何时有效性的理解。论文旨在建立可测量的动力学属性来预测和保证深度安全性。

Method: 提出将训练操作符的有限时间动力学机制估计为settling、marginal或drifting三种状态；推导深度安全性的充分条件（每步位移小到相对解码器边际可忽略时答案不变）；引入单一终端不动点目标来调节动力学机制；给出四个操作性判据用于评估测试时深度的有效性并编目失败模式。

Result: 在从每难度层级800个未增强样本训练的算法任务上，settling操作符不会随深度增加而退化，在某些任务上对更难未见实例带来准确率提升（Sudoku从0.19到0.34）。移除终端不动点目标会导致漂移并消除增益，而添加该目标到通用递推可在进位传播上实现深度安全的外推。Huginn-3.5B落入非settling族。

Conclusion: 训练操作符的动力学机制是预测测试时深度行为的关键可测量属性，终端不动点目标是实现深度安全外推的有效手段，为设计深度安全的递归推理器提供了可操作准则。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth reasoners aim to solve harder problems by iterating their update longer at test time, but additional iterations can improve, preserve, or degrade an answer. We show that a measurable property of the trained operator, its finite-time dynamical regime (estimated as settling, marginal, or drifting), indicates which of these occurs. We give a sufficient condition for depth-safety: once an operator's per-step displacement is small relative to the decoder margin, the decoded answer cannot change under further iterations. Empirically, on algorithmic tasks trained from $800$ unaugmented examples per difficulty tier, settling operators do not degrade with added depth, and on some tasks convert it into higher accuracy on harder unseen instances (Sudoku, $0.19$ to $0.34$ past the training horizon). A single terminal fixed-point objective moves the regime and the depth behavior together: removing it induces drift and removes the gains, and adding it to a generic recurrence yields depth-safe extrapolation on carry propagation. We give four operational criteria for useful test-time depth, use them to catalogue failure modes, and, as a consistency check, apply the same measurements to Huginn-3.5B, which falls in the non-settling family.

</details>


### [188] [Allocating Recurrent Compute in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18230)
*Ruhai Lin,Yiyang Guo,Rui-Jie Zhu,Hao Ye,Jason K. Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MixerLoop，一种仅循环重复Gated DeltaNet混频器而将密集FFN仅执行一次的循环语言模型架构，在15M和110M参数规模下与无循环和全块循环对比，MixerLoop在减少45.9%循环骨干网络投影FLOPs的同时，在15M规模超越全块循环，在110M规模保留了41.5%的CORE提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环语言模型通常重复执行整个层堆栈，但混频器和密集FFN执行不同操作且成本不同。作者质疑应该循环什么，认为有价值的循环应能暴露新的跨位置影响方向，希望通过精确分析循环各组件的作用来降低计算成本而不损失性能。

Method: 提出迭代传输秩（ITR）和边际ITR来刻画累积影响力轨迹与非冗余贡献；基于此设计MixerLoop架构，仅循环重复Gated DeltaNet混频器，密集FFN只执行一次；采用有限上下文化干预测试验证后续混频器应用是否在最终语言模型读出端产生独特且有价值的改变；在相同数据、初始化和架构下于15M和110M参数规模进行对比实验。

Result: MixerLoop在15M规模下在CORE聚合指标上超越FullLoop；在110M规模下保留了FullLoop 41.5%的CORE改进，同时将循环骨干网络的投影FLOPs减少了45.9%。

Conclusion: 循环深度的收益可以在不重复执行密集FFN的情况下保留，这验证了MixerLoop的有效性，表明循环语言模型中应针对性地循环重复混频器而非整个层堆栈。

Abstract: Looped language models improve reasoning and knowledge manipulation by applying shared computation repeatedly. Existing systems usually repeat an entire layer stack, although a mixer and a dense feed-forward network (FFN) perform different operations and have different costs. We ask a narrower question: what should loop? We view recurrence as repeated composition of a state update and argue that an application is valuable when it exposes a new cross-position influence direction that remains observable at the task readout. Iterative Transport Rank (ITR) describes the cumulative influence trajectory; marginal ITR describes the nonredundant influence contributed by successive applications. This view motivates MixerLoop, which repeats each Gated DeltaNet mixer while applying its dense FFN once. We compare MixerLoop with no recurrence and full-block recurrence at 15M and 110M parameters under the same data, initialization, and architecture. A finite context-off intervention tests whether later mixer applications produce distinct, non-negligible, and beneficial changes at the final language-model readout. MixerLoop surpasses FullLoop on aggregate CORE at 15M and retains 41.5% of its CORE improvement at 110M while reducing recurrent-backbone projection FLOPs by 45.9%. These results show that the benefits of recurrent depth can be retained without repeatedly executing the dense FFN.

</details>


### [189] [Classifying Directional Trajectories Near Criticality in the Three-State Majority-Vote Model with Deep Belief Networks and Bidirectional GRUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18235)
*Mauricio A. Valle,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度信念网络（DBN）和双向门控循环单元（Bi-GRU）能否区分三态多数投票模型（MV3）中四种动态轨迹类型（无序接近、有序接近、向无序离开、向有序离开）。DBN通过无监督预训练将晶格快照编码为81维潜在向量，但t-SNE显示其仅能部分区分四种轨迹；而基于DBN编码快照序列训练的两层Bi-GRU分类器则实现了近乎完美的分离，并能通过滑动窗口实时感知系统当前动力学状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于智能体的舆论动力学模型中，检测和分类临界转变（critical transitions）是一个重要但具有挑战性的问题。静态配置训练得到的模型无法充分捕捉轨迹的时间方向性结构，因此需要一个能同时利用空间和时间信息的层次化架构来区分不同的动力学期轨迹类型。

Method: 采用两阶段层次架构：（1）DBN（784→4096→225→81）通过高斯-伯努利受限玻尔兹曼机输入层，在静态平衡样本上进行无监督预训练，将每个晶格快照编码为81维潜在向量；（2）在长度为T=50的DBN编码快照序列上训练两层Bi-GRU分类器，用于区分四种轨迹类型，并通过t-SNE进行可视化验证。

Result: DBN潜在空间仅能部分分离四种轨迹类型，说明静态模型无法充分解析方向性时间结构；两层Bi-GRU分类器在训练集和测试集的隐藏状态空间中均实现了近乎完美的四分类分离；滑动窗口应用Bi-GRU能够实时感知连续MV3动力学中系统的当前动态状态。

Conclusion: 所提出的层次化架构（DBN+Bi-GRU）为在基于智能体的舆论动力学模型中检测和分类临界转变建立了一种原理性的方法，结合了无监督空间特征提取和监督时序分类的优势。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate whether the latent representations learned by a Deep Belief Network (DBN) and a Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit (Bi-GRU) can discriminate among four dynamically distinct trajectory types in the three-state majority vote model (MV3): approach from disorder, approach from order, departure to disorder, and departure to order. The DBN, pre-trained in an unsupervised manner on static equilibrium samples via a Gaussian-Bernoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine input layer and architecture $784 \to 4096 \to 225 \to 81$, encodes each lattice snapshot into an 81-dimensional latent vector. A t-SNE analysis of the DBN latent space reveals only partial separation of the four trajectory types, reflecting the fact that a model trained on static configurations cannot fully resolve directional temporal structure. A two-layer Bi-GRU classifier, trained on sequences of DBN-encoded snapshots of length $T = 50$, achieves near-perfect separation of all four trajectory types in its hidden state space, as confirmed by t-SNE visualization on both training and test sets. Furthermore, a sliding-window application of the trained Bi-GRU to continuous MV3 dynamics demonstrates its ability to sense the system's current dynamical regime in real-time. These results establish a principled hierarchical architecture for detecting and classifying critical transitions in agent-based opinion dynamics models.

</details>


### [190] [ClosureBench: A Constructive Benchmark for Compositional Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18242)
*Stefano Goria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ClosureBench——一个基于Ein张量逻辑语言、通过程序执行生成实例的构造性图关系推理基准，解决了固定测试集受数据污染的问题。实验发现：模型在seen/fresh实例上存在19.3个百分点的能力差距；图规模与查询深度交互导致最强模型性能下降，且与输入格式无关；4B模型通过程序合成而非直接预测答案，在组合层级上保持稳定并接近前沿模型94.3%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图关系推理基准采用固定测试集，存在数据污染风险，无法直接测量模型的记忆化程度。需要一种能按需生成新实例、且参考答案可机器验证的构造性基准来更可靠地评估模型的组合推理能力。

Method: 构建ClosureBench基准：基于Ein张量逻辑语言定义26个任务类别，分为三个组合推理层级(L1-L3)，沿图规模、边密度和查询深度三个轴控制难度，通过执行程序生成机器可验证的参考答案。在此基础上对从1.5B到前沿系统的多类模型进行评估，并设计三种输入格式（散文、JSON边列表、邻接矩阵）以隔离推理瓶颈。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1) 在固定测试集上微调的模型在seen与fresh实例间存在19.3个百分点准确率差距，静态基准无法揭示此现象；(2) 图规模和查询深度增加时准确率下降且两者存在交互——模型在理解图的自然语言描述时出错，但推理过程本身正确，该瓶颈在JSON和邻接矩阵输入下依然存在；(3) 4B模型通过输出可执行程序而非答案进行微调，在组合层级间保持平稳，以较低token成本达到94.3%的准确率，且对Ein和Python+NetworkX两种程序目标均成立。

Conclusion: ClosureBench证明构造性基准能直接量化记忆化程度；图关系推理的主要瓶颈在于模型从语言描述中准确构造图结构的能力而非组合推理本身；程序合成是提升小模型组合推理能力的有效且语言无关的途径。

Abstract: We introduce ClosureBench, a constructive benchmark for compositional graph-relational reasoning with programmatically verified ground truth. Unlike fixed-test-set benchmarks vulnerable to data contamination, ClosureBench generates instances on demand: each task's reference answer is computed by executing a program in the Ein tensor-logic language, ensuring machine-verified correctness. The benchmark spans 26 task categories at three compositional levels (L1-L3), with difficulty controlled along three independent axes: graph size, edge density, and query depth.
  We evaluate models from 1.5B open weights to frontier systems (o3, GPT-4.1, Gemini 2.5, Claude Sonnet 4) and report three findings. First, because the benchmark can always supply fresh instances, it measures memorisation directly: a model fine-tuned on a fixed test set shows a 19.3 percentage-point gap between its accuracy on seen and on fresh instances, which a static test set cannot reveal. We scope this to supervised fine-tuning on answer pairs, not pretraining contamination. Second, accuracy falls as graph size and query depth increase, and the two interact: models misread the graph from its natural-language description and then reason correctly over the wrong graph, so even the strongest frontier model degrades from atomic to compositional queries. This bottleneck is a property of the reasoning rather than the input format: it persists when the graph is given as a JSON edge list or an adjacency matrix instead of prose. Third, a 4B model fine-tuned to emit executable programs rather than answers stays nearly flat across compositional levels and approaches frontier accuracy (94.3% on held-out instances) at a fraction of the token cost. This holds for two program targets, Ein and Python+NetworkX, so it is a property of verified program synthesis rather than of one language.

</details>


### [191] [Bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18244)
*Samuel Kostousov,Abhinn Kaushik,Brokoslaw Laschowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现生物与人工神经网络的表征对齐存在不对称性（模型预测神经反应远好于神经预测模型）。作者提出通过谱正则化在训练中调控表征几何形状，可系统性地改善双向对齐。在自监督对比视觉模型上，该方法使反向预测性显著提升，双向预测性相对提高55%，且中间谱指数下正反向预测性趋于对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究揭示生物-人工神经网络表征对齐存在方向性不对称：模型表征预测神经响应的能力远强于神经响应预测模型表征的能力。这种不对称性引发了关于表征几何是否影响双向对齐的疑问，作者试图探究能否通过调控表征几何来系统性地改善双向对齐。

Method: 开发了一个将谱正则化与双向预测性分析相结合的计算框架。在训练过程中通过调节表征的谱几何（即特征值分布/有效维度），引导模型表征的几何结构，随后对自监督对比视觉模型进行双向预测性评估。

Result: 调控谱几何显著提升了反向预测性，仅带来较小的正向预测性损失；双向预测性相对提升55%。改进伴随有效维度降低和共享表征子空间的重组，在中间谱指数下正向与反向预测性趋于近似对称。

Conclusion: 表征几何可以被系统地调控以调节生物与人工神经网络之间的双向表征对齐，揭示了表征几何在对齐不对称性中的关键作用，为构建更对称的神经-模型对齐提供了新的调控途径。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks is asymmetric: model representations predict neural responses much better than neural responses predict model representations. This asymmetry raises the question of whether representational geometry contributes to bidirectional representational alignment. We hypothesized that steering representational geometry during training can systematically influence bidirectional alignment. To test this hypothesis, we developed a computational framework that integrates spectral regularization with bidirectional predictivity analyses. As an initial demonstration, we evaluated our framework using self-supervised contrastive vision models. Steering the spectral geometry of the learned representations substantially increased reverse predictivity with modest reductions in forward predictivity, yielding a 55% relative improvement in bidirectional predictivity. These improvements were accompanied by reduced effective dimensionality and a reorganization of the shared representational subspace, within which forward and reverse predictivity became approximately symmetric at intermediate spectral exponents. Overall, these findings demonstrate that representational geometry can be systematically steered to modulate bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks.

</details>


### [192] [SIGMA: Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive Control for Robust, Dependable Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18263)
*Pratham Payra,Jagadish B,Tanmay Sen,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIGMA是一个结合大语言模型与强化学习的交通信号控制框架，通过自然语言指令生成多目标优先级向量、旋转增广提升跨路口泛化能力，并在SUMO仿真中优于固定配时、感应控制和DQN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习交通信号控制方法存在固定目标函数、无法处理动态优先级变化、难以在不同几何相似路口间泛化等问题，需要一种能自适应目标调整且具有泛化能力的框架。

Method: 提出SIGMA框架：利用LLM将自然语言应急指令转换为多目标actor-critic控制器的优先级向量；采用旋转增广提升四向路口间的迁移能力；结合离线到在线学习实现稳定初始化与渐进适应；定义可靠性属性（应急服务等级、LLM故障时的优雅降级、需求敏感性），并用bootstrap统计验证。

Result: 在SUMO中对加尔各答四个城市路口进行仿真，相较于固定配时、感应控制和DQN控制器，SIGMA降低了平均/应急等待时间和队列长度，提升了吞吐量；消融实验验证了其对组件故障和几何旋转的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SIGMA提供了一种可靠、语言引导、多目标的交通信号控制系统，具备统计可靠性保证，有效解决了动态优先级调整和跨路口泛化两大核心挑战。

Abstract: Traffic signal control is a complex sequential decision-making problem requiring real-time adaptation and trade-offs among throughput, delay fairness, signal stability, and emergency vehicle priority. Existing RL methods often fix objectives, ignore dynamic priority changes, and fail to generalize across geometrically similar intersections.We propose SIGMA (Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive traffic control), an RL framework enhanced with a large language model (LLM) for adaptive objective tuning and orientation-invariant learning. SIGMA converts natural-language emergency commands into priority vectors for a multi-objective actor-critic controller, avoiding manual reward engineering. Rotational augmentation improves transferability across four-way intersections, while offline-to-online learning ensures stable initialization and gradual adaptation to changing traffic.We define reliability properties covering emergency service levels, graceful degradation under LLM failures, and demand sensitivity, validated via bootstrap statistics. Evaluated in SUMO on four Kolkata-based urban intersections against fixed-time, actuated, and DQN controllers, SIGMA reduces average/emergency waiting times and queue lengths, and boosts throughput. Ablation studies confirm robustness to component failures and geometric rotations. Overall, SIGMA offers a reliable, language-guided, multi-objective traffic control system with statistical reliability assurance.

</details>


### [193] [Rethinking Privileged Information in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18271)
*Samyak Shrestha,Alexander Tessier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线自我蒸馏（OPSD）中性能提升的来源，挑战了"学生从特权参考信息中学到了新知识"这一假设。通过在Qwen3模型（1.7B-8B）上对科学和数学数据集的实验，发现正确参考信息并未带来一致性的性能提升，学生模型的改进更多来源于基座模型已有的推理行为，而非参考信息本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD声称通过特权参考信息的token级监督来训练学生模型，但论文质疑这种性能提升究竟是因为学生真正学到了参考信息中的知识，还是仅仅恢复了基座模型已有的推理能力。这一问题对于理解自我蒸馏方法的本质机制至关重要。

Method: 论文构建了一个分析框架，将参考信息诱导的监督与无参考的教师监督分离，分别测量二者与学生预测变化的一致性。使用Qwen3模型（1.7B-8B）在科学和数学数据集上进行了OPSD实验，并设置了多种对照组（包括使用其他问题的解作为参考）来隔离参考信息的因果效应。

Result: 实验发现：（1）正确参考信息在不同教师生成模式、模型规模和训练数据集下无法提供一致性的性能收益；（2）没有正确参考信息学生也能提升，甚至其他问题的解在多个数学推理基准上可以优于正确解；（3）学生预测与基座模型思考行为的一致性高于与参考信息监督的一致性，但来自其他问题的对照也能复现大部分这种一致性；（4）更强的一致性并不与更大的性能收益可靠对应。

Conclusion: 仅通过性能提升和分布对齐无法确定特权参考信息在OPSD中如何促进学生学习。OPSD的收益可能主要来自基座模型已有的推理能力，而非参考信息提供的额外知识。这提醒研究者需要更严谨的因果分析来评估自我蒸馏方法中各种组件的作用。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own responses using token-level supervision from the same model conditioned on privileged reference information. We investigate whether performance gains from OPSD show that the student learned the information in the reference or instead reflect recovery of reasoning behavior already present in the base model. We perform OPSD experiments on science and mathematics datasets using Qwen3 models ranging from 1.7B to 8B. Our analysis framework separates the supervision induced by the reference from the supervision provided by the teacher without the reference and measures how each aligns with changes in the student's predictions. The correct reference does not provide a consistent performance benefit across teacher generation modes, model sizes, and training datasets. Students can improve without the correct reference, and a solution from another problem can outperform the correct solution on several mathematical reasoning benchmarks. The student's predictions align more strongly with the base model's thinking behavior than with the supervision induced by the reference, but controls constructed from other problems reproduce much of both alignments. Moreover, stronger alignment attributable to the correct reference does not reliably coincide with a greater performance benefit from the reference. Performance gains and distributional alignment alone therefore cannot determine how privileged reference information contributes to student learning in OPSD.

</details>


### [194] [SingularClip: Preventing Spectral Collapse to Maintain Plasticity in Continual and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18319)
*Tyler Kastner,Nimrod De La Vega,Amir-massoud Farahmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现神经网络在非平稳任务训练中由于权重矩阵奇异值各向异性增长导致可塑性丧失，并提出SingularClip方法通过周期性裁剪奇异值来缓解该问题，在持续监督学习和深度强化学习任务中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在非平稳任务（持续学习）中会丧失适应新目标的能力（即可塑性丧失），而现有的可塑性丧失原因分析尚不完整，需要识别新的机制并提出有效的缓解方法。

Method: 作者首先从经验上和理论上分析了权重矩阵奇异值各向异性增长这一新颖的可塑性丧失来源，然后提出SingularClip方法，即周期性对所有权重矩阵的奇异值进行裁剪（clipping），以遏制各向异性增长。

Result: SingularClip在持续监督学习和深度强化学习的一系列任务中均优于基线方法，验证了该方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 权重矩阵奇异值各向异性是导致神经网络可塑性丧失的一个重要原因，SingularClip通过简单有效的奇异值裁剪机制可以显著提升模型在非平稳任务上的持续学习能力。

Abstract: Neural networks trained on nonstationary tasks frequently lose the ability to fit new targets, a phenomenon referred to as loss of plasticity. We identify a novel source of plasticity loss due to the growing anisotropy of weight matrices' singular values during training, and analyze this phenomenon both empirically and theoretically. To mitigate this issue, we introduce SingularClip, a procedure that periodically clips the singular values of all weight matrices. We show that SingularClip performs strongly against baselines across a range of tasks in both continual supervised learning and deep reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [195] [When Does Dynamic Ensembling Pay Off? Diagnosing Regionwise Gains in Regression under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18330)
*Tianxin Zhou,Ruixi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出D_CF5估计量，利用小规模带标注的目标域探针数据，预测"分区域动态回归模型组合"相比"最佳静态混合"的增益是否值得部署。在12个数据集-偏移组合上，预测与实测增益的Spearman相关系数达+0.98，并提出了带门控机制的Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector进行动态部署决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回归模型池的动态（输入依赖）组合是否优于静态混合，取决于具体的分布偏移类型，且在部署前通常未知。作者希望通过一个小型带标注的目标域探针集，提前判断"按输入空间区域重新分配信任"是否能带来收益，从而避免盲目部署。

Method: 提出D_CF5估计量，从探针数据中交叉拟合估计"区域凸组合"相对"最优静态凸混合"的增益；进而提出Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector，使用留出置信下界作为门控，只有在超过静态凸组合基线时才部署动态候选模型。同时发布OpenRegShift评估框架。

Result: 在12个数据集-偏移对上，D_CF5预测与实测区域测试增益的Spearman为+0.98（95% CI [+0.83, +1.00]，p=5e-5）；16对敏感性分析中为+0.83，优于替代探针诊断（最高+0.66）。对照实验显示对区域凸组合相关性+0.98，而对平滑协变量依赖stacking仅+0.01。门控部署中12次运行全部匹配或改善静态基线，两次部署降低测试风险11%和16%，未门控部署则遭遇>30倍静态损失。

Conclusion: 小规模探针数据足以可靠地预测区域级动态模型组合的部署价值；门控机制能有效防止有害部署。动态增益源于偏移异质性与局部能力的交互，且随偏移严重程度增加，在128-256个探针标签时可实现。

Abstract: Whether input-dependent ("dynamic") combination of a regression model pool beats the best static blend depends on the shift and is rarely known before deployment. Can a small labeled target-domain probe tell us when reallocating trust across regions of the input space will pay off? We answer this with $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$, which estimates from the probe the cross-fitted gain of the regionwise convex combination over the best static convex blend: the realizable value of deciding, region by region, whom to trust. Across a frozen suite of 12 dataset-shift pairs (spatial, temporal, domain, feature-cluster), $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$ predicts realized regionwise test gains with dataset-level Spearman $+0.98$ (95% CI $[+0.83, +1.00]$; $p=5\times10^{-5}$), including two cases overturning preregistered expectations. The relationship holds in a 16-pair sensitivity analysis (Spearman $+0.83$), whereas alternative probe diagnostics reach at most $+0.66$. This contrast isolates regional trust reallocation: correlation is $+0.98$ for regionwise-convex gain, but $+0.01$ for smooth covariate-dependent stacking after affine correction. A controlled generator shows dynamic gains arise from the interaction of shift heterogeneity and local competence, increase with shift severity, and become realizable between 128 and 256 probe labels in the tested grid. The Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector chooses among a static affine stacker and dynamic realizers, deploying a candidate only when a held-out lower confidence bound clears the static-convex floor. In a preregistered prospective batch, it matched or improved the floor in all 12 runs; two deployments reduced test risk by 11% and 16%, while the gate rejected a candidate whose un-gated deployment incurred $>30\times$ the static loss. We release OpenRegShift, a reproducible evaluation harness for regression ensembles under distribution shift.

</details>


### [196] [Selection, Recombination, or a Fresh Solve? A Candidate-Free Control for Single-Pass Test-Time Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18379)
*Guiv Farmanfarmaian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究引入缺失的候选无关对照（同最大输出token预算），在AIME-2025与HMMT-2025上使用Qwen3-4B评估候选条件化聚合的价值。发现多个候选正确时条件化提升准确率（+0.290），全部候选错误时反而降低准确率（-0.123），单一正确候选时结论不明确。矫正多重比较后，c2+与c0的结论依然稳健。在全部错误场景下，条件化于错误候选池相比直接重新求解反而更差，颠覆了"全错恢复"的直觉解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的候选聚合研究缺乏合适的对照基准——当所有候选都错误时无法做"正确候选选择"，但聚合仍可能重新求解获得正确答案。关键在于：候选上下文是否在额外生成次数之外带来增量价值？此前缺少在同一输出token预算下的候选无关控制实验。

Method: 引入候选无关控制组（同最大输出token额度），按正确候选数量分层（c0、c1、c2+），在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025上使用Qwen3-4B进行单次提示聚合，并对双基准自适应程序做保守多重比较矫正；另设计结构化干预（显式答案字段、掩码）及placebo实验探究失效机制。

Result: c2+时候选条件化提升准确率+0.290；c0时降低-0.123；c1时结果不确定。矫正多重比较后c2+和c0的结论仍稳健。全错场景下，候选条件化优于新鲜求解的解读被逆转。显式答案字段会因果性地将输出导向字段值，掩码无显著改善，且与原格式的等价性未确立。

Conclusion: 候选上下文的价值高度依赖于正确候选数量：多正确时有益，全错时有害。该证据局限于Qwen3-4B家族、两个数学基准、首答截断的候选片段及单次提示聚合，机制仍未解开。

Abstract: When every candidate is wrong, correct-candidate selection is unavailable, yet the aggregation call can still solve the problem afresh. A correct aggregate answer may therefore reflect recombination, fresh solving, or both. For efficient test-time reasoning, the relevant question is whether candidate context adds value beyond the additional generation pass. We introduce the missing candidate-free control under the same maximum output-token allowance and stratify by the number of correct candidates. Across AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025 with Qwen3-4B, candidate conditioning improves accuracy when multiple candidates are correct ($Δ_{\mathrm{cand}}$(c2+) = +0.290), lowers accuracy when every candidate is wrong ($Δ_{\mathrm{cand}}$(c0) = -0.123), and remains unresolved in the one-correct regime. The c2+ and c0 conclusions survive a conservative correction for the adaptive two-benchmark procedure. Under this counterfactual, the interpretation of all-wrong recovery reverses at this scale: conditioning on an all-wrong candidate pool lowers accuracy relative to a fresh solve. Original-format matching and placebo results characterize the failures descriptively but leave their mechanism unresolved. Within a separate structured intervention, explicit answer fields causally steer outputs toward their values; masking yields no measurable accuracy improvement, and equivalence with the original format was not established. The evidence is limited to one Qwen3-4B family, two mathematics benchmarks, first-answer-truncated candidate fragments, and single-pass prompted aggregation.

</details>


### [197] [Vector Symbolic Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18404)
*Ryozo Masukawa,Sanggeon Yun,SungHeon Jeong,Hyunwoo Oh,Raheeb Hassan,Pietro Mercati,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Mahdi Imani,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出向量符号策略梯度（VSPG），一种基于向量符号架构（VSA）的离散动作actor，将每个动作表示为单位范数超向量，通过相似度对编码状态进行打分。作者证明其在标准softmax策略梯度代理下的更新等价于优势加权超向量捆绑后归一化，并进一步证明训练后的动作超向量是固定大小的压缩核记忆，存储优势加权核展开并在状态间传递证据。对于双极动作记忆，还证明了贪心动作选择在随机比特翻转下具有指数级衰减的失败概率稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以将向量符号架构（VSA）的优势用于强化学习中的动作记忆，从而实现样本高效学习且不增加推理内存开销，同时获得定量的鲁棒性保证。

Method: 提出VSPG框架：将每个动作编码为单位范数超向量，通过超向量与编码状态的相似度进行动作打分；证明其策略梯度更新恰好等价于优势加权超向量捆绑后归一化；将动作超向量解释为固定大小的压缩核记忆；对双极动作记忆给出了鲁棒性的理论分析。

Result: 1) 证明了VSPG更新与优势加权捆绑精确等价，兼容标准优势估计器；2) 每个动作超向量可视为压缩核记忆，支持样本高效学习且推理内存固定；3) 双极动作记忆在随机比特翻转下贪心选择失败概率随超向量维度指数衰减，提供了定量鲁棒性保证。

Conclusion: VSPG统一了VSA动作记忆、log-linear策略梯度和核策略搜索三种视角，同时提供了量化鲁棒性保证，为VSA在强化学习中的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We answer this question with Vector-Symbolic Policy Gradient (VSPG), a discrete-action actor that represents each action by a unit-norm hypervector and scores it by similarity to the encoded state. Under the standard softmax policy-gradient surrogate, we prove that its update is exactly advantage-weighted hypervector bundling followed by normalization, and therefore supports standard advantage estimators. We further show that each trained action hypervector is a fixed-size compressed kernel memory, storing an advantage-weighted kernel expansion over visited states and transferring evidence according to the encoder-induced similarity. This provides a concrete mechanism that can support sample-efficient learning without increasing inference-time memory. Finally, for bipolar action memories, we prove that greedy action selection is stable under random bit flips, with failure probability decaying exponentially in the hypervector dimension. VSPG thus connects VSA action memories, log-linear policy gradients, and kernel policy search while providing a quantitative robustness guarantee.

</details>


### [198] [Role-Conditioned Sub-Token Routing for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18410)
*Wei Jiang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RoleSub方法，通过对保留token的价值表示进行子token压缩，在视觉和语言两个模态上实现VLA模型的轻量化推理，在LIBERO基准测试中大幅压缩记忆需求的同时保持控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型处理长多模态token序列，推理成本高昂。现有方法主要依赖视觉token剪枝，但激进剪枝会丢失整个token的表示信息，导致性能脆弱。子token压缩作为补充方案在VLA策略中直接应用效果不佳，因为感知、语言理解与控制所需信息在多模态表示中分布不均。

Method: RoleSub在视觉token缩减之后，将每个保留token的价值表示划分到正交空间中的多个组，并通过轻量路由器决定保留哪些组。路由决策由token表示、学习到的潜在角色表示和语言上下文共同条件化。该机制同样适用于语言值的压缩，实现视觉和语言表示在不删除额外token的情况下被压缩。

Result: 在OpenVLA-OFT-7B上的四个LIBERO基准套件中，匹配视觉KV预算下，RoleSub在36个设置中的33个优于仅token剪枝的对照方法，在激进压缩下收益最大。结合视觉和语言压缩，可将总KV减少至原始的9.2%-11.3%，且在大多数任务上保持强控制性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在保留token内部减少表示维度是token剪枝的有效补充策略，能够在激进压缩VLA模型时保持性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models process long multimodal token sequences, making inference expensive in both memory and computation. Existing efficiency methods mainly reduce visual tokens, but aggressive token pruning becomes fragile because removing a token discards its entire representation. Sub-token compression provides a complementary alternative by retaining more tokens while reducing their value width. However, directly applying sub-token compression to VLA policies is less effective because information important for perception, language understanding, and control is distributed differently across the multimodal representation.
  We introduce Role-Conditioned Sub-Token Routing (RoleSub), which learns how to compress the value representations of retained tokens. After visual token reduction, RoleSub partitions each retained value representation into groups in an orthogonal space and uses a lightweight router to determine which groups should be preserved. The routing decision is conditioned on the token representation, a learned latent role representation, and language context. The same mechanism can also be applied to language values, allowing visual and language representations to be compressed without removing additional tokens.
  We evaluate RoleSub on OpenVLA-OFT-7B across the four LIBERO suites. At matched visual-KV budgets, RoleSub outperforms a trained token-only control in 33 of 36 settings, with the largest gains under aggressive compression. Combining visual and language compression reduces total KV to 9.2--11.3% of the original while retaining strong control performance on most tasks. These results show that reducing the representation within retained tokens provides an effective complement to token pruning for aggressive VLA compression.

</details>


### [199] [The Road Taken: The Role of Optimizers at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18415)
*Jaerin Lee,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"实现边缘稳定性"（realized edge of stability）的新框架，指出传统基于最大Hessian特征值的稳定性理论在实际第一阶优化器中严重失效（偏差可达×21.1），并通过引入方向Hessian和梯度对齐分数，基于优化器实际更新方向重新推导稳定性阈值，从而消除优化器相关的系统偏移，并提供新的诊断工具揭示优化器在时空预算平衡中的角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘稳定性理论基于最大Hessian特征值和学习率来预测稳定性边界，但实验发现许多一阶优化器（包括梯度下降）显著违反这些预测（偏差达21.1倍），且这种偏差是系统性的并高度依赖底层优化器，现有理论框架无法解释这一现象。

Method: 从方向Hessian和梯度对齐分数出发，基于优化器实际采取的更新方向（而非最大曲率模态）推导新的稳定性阈值，形成"实现边缘稳定性"的新公式化框架。

Result: 新公式消除了与优化器相关的系统偏移，提供更一致的稳定性阈值预测，并引入了新的诊断工具，揭示了优化器在第一阶优化中主动平衡时间预算与空间预算的独特作用。

Conclusion: 边缘稳定性不应仅由最大Hessian特征值定义，而应基于优化器实际更新方向的方向Hessian来衡量。该框架不仅更准确地预测稳定性阈值，还加深了对不同优化器行为差异的理解。

Abstract: The edge of stability refers to a phenomenon in deep learning with gradient-based optimizers where the Hessian eigenvalues of the loss remain stable above a threshold that the classical descent lemma predicts to be unstable. Previous works formulate the edge of stability with respect to the maximum Hessian eigenvalue and the learning rate. However, we observe that many first-order methods, including gradient descent, significantly violate the stability bound predicted by these theories by a factor as large as $\times 21.1$. Moreover, this deviation turns out to be systematic and highly dependent on the underlying optimizer, which is not captured by previous formulations. This calls for a new formulation of the stability threshold, which we derive from the directional Hessian and the gradient-alignment score with respect to the actual update taken by the optimizer, rather than the maximum curvature mode. Our new formulation of the realized edge of stability not only removes optimizer-dependent offsets and provides more consistent predictions of the stability threshold, but also introduces new diagnostic tools that reveal the unique role of the optimizer in actively balancing between the temporal and spatial budgets in first-order optimization.

</details>


### [200] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Structure-Aware Numerical Reasoning in LLaMA 3.1 8B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18419)
*Rahul Chowdhury,Timothy A Rupprecht,Senhao Cao,Jiahao Liu,Octavia Camps,David Bau,Pu Zhao,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从机制可解释性角度研究Llama 3.1-8B如何实现时间序列预测，发现在无需显式监督的情况下，模型能在内部表示中计算并存储一阶差分，并通过类似归纳回路的机制检索相关差分值并加到当前值上，从而完成预测任务。这是首批在LLM中发现概念归纳形式的研究之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在数值序列建模和时间序列预测上表现出色，但其背后的工作机制尚不清楚。作者希望探究模型是否真正理解序列的底层结构（至少需要对序列中数字的一阶差分进行推理），而不仅仅是表面模式匹配。

Method: 作者设计了一个必须依赖结构线索才能解决的序列建模任务：采样n个随机数并以偏移量重复。采用机制可解释性方法，包括探针实验和基于激活修补的反事实分析，来剖析Llama 3.1-8B的内部计算过程。

Result: Llama在任务上表现优异，表明其能捕捉底层结构。探针实验显示模型无需显式监督即可在内部表示中计算并存储一阶差分；激活修补揭示模型通过类似归纳回路的机制检索所需的一阶差分，并将其加到当前值上完成预测。

Conclusion: 这项工作揭示了LLM在数值序列预测中的一种具体机制——概念归纳（concept induction），证明模型确实在内部跟踪序列的结构信息，而非仅进行表面模式学习，为理解LLM的时间序列推理能力提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong numerical sequence modeling capabilities and show promise in time-series prediction. While LLMs display in-context learning capabilities, the mechanisms with which they accomplish time-series prediction remain unclear. Specifically, whether they truly understand the underlying structure, which at a minimum requires reasoning over first differences in the sequence of numbers. To study this, we investigate Llama 3.1-8B from a mechanistic interpretability point of view. Mechanistic interpretability is an emerging field concerned with the reverse engineering of the algorithms learned by neural networks such as LLMs. To assess Llamas' numerical sequence modeling capabilities and to facilitate our mechanistic interpretability analysis, we create a sequence modeling task that cannot be solved without picking up structural cues. Specifically, we sample n random numbers and repeat them with an offset. We find that Llama displays strong performance on our tasks suggesting that it can pick up on the underlying structure. To understand the mechanisms that allow it to do so, we perform probing experiments and activation patching based counterfactual analysis. Probing reveals that the model computes and stores first differences in its internal representations without explicit supervision, indicating that it tracks structural information about the sequence. Activation patching reveals that Llama retrieves the relevant first-difference with a mechanism similar to an induction circuit and subsequently adds it to the current value. Notably, our work represents one of the first studies to identify this form of concept induction in LLMs.

</details>


### [201] [Adaptive Multi-Agent Feature Selection for Personalized Fall Risk Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18450)
*Chang Liu,Ladda Thiamwong,Yanjie Fu,Rui Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PAFIR框架，将自适应特征选择建模为强化学习问题，应用于老年人的纵向多模态健康数据，以识别跌倒风险因素。该框架同时建模评估变量间的结构依赖关系、可穿戴设备衍生的身体活动数据的时间动态，并利用稀疏跌倒事件的奖励信号学习自适应选择策略。在PEER随机对照试验数据上的实验表明，PAFIR比现有基线方法更有效地捕获特征相关性的纵向和结构模式，实现动态、个体化的特征选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒是一个复杂的公共健康问题，涉及多个风险域之间随时间变化的复杂交互。现有跌倒风险因素识别方法大多是静态的，无法自适应地对跨模态和跨时间的个体化风险因素进行演化建模。同时，跌倒相关结果事件稀疏且延迟，进一步增加了建模难度。

Method: PAFIR将自适应特征选择形式化为强化学习问题，应用于纵向多模态健康数据。它联合建模相关评估变量之间的结构依赖关系和可穿戴设备衍生的身体活动数据的时间动态，并利用来自稀疏跌倒发生结果的奖励信号，在多次研究访视中学习自适应选择策略。

Result: 在PEER整群随机对照试验数据上的实验结果表明，PAFIR比最先进的基线方法更能捕获特征相关性的纵向和结构模式，并实现了动态、个体化的特征选择。

Conclusion: 通过随时间自适应地选择特征，PAFIR支持更及时和个性化的跌倒预防策略。

Abstract: Falls among older adults represent a major public health challenge driven by complex, time-varying interactions across multiple risk domains. Effective fall risk factor identification requires learning from heterogeneous longitudinal data while accounting for sparse and delayed fall-related outcome events. However, existing approaches are largely static and fail to adaptively model evolving, individualized risk factors across modalities and time. We propose PAFIR, a Personalized and Adaptive Feature selection framework for fall risk Identification and pRevention, which formulates adaptive feature selection as a reinforcement learning problem over longitudinal multimodal health data. PAFIR jointly models structural dependencies among correlated assessment variables and temporal dynamics in wearable-derived physical activity data, and learns adaptive selection policies across repeated study visits using reward signals derived from sparse fall incidence outcomes. We apply PAFIR to data from the Physio fEedback Exercise pRogram (PEER) cluster-randomized trial. Experimental results demonstrate that PAFIR more effectively captures longitudinal and structural patterns of feature relevance than state-of-the-art baselines, and enables dynamic, subject-specific feature selection. By adapting selected features over time, PAFIR supports more timely and personalized fall prevention strategies.

</details>


### [202] [Atrial Fibrillation Detection with Arbitrary Leads via a Codebook-Based Reconstruction-Classification Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18451)
*Hongtao Li,Jia Wei,Guoyao Li,Yuchen Lei,Guangnian Ma,Jia Xiao,Yuanjun Lai,Shuzhen Lv,Xueqiang Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DCGCNet，一种端到端的向量量化变分自编码器网络，通过局部-全局对比学习模块和自适应码本向量量化器，实现了跨数据集、抗噪声的心房颤动（AF）检测，在七种跨数据集场景下AUC均超过0.98。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床环境下，心房颤动检测面临导联配置多变、跨数据集域偏移以及生理和技术伪影等挑战，现有模型鲁棒性和泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出双码本图协作网络（DCGCNet），采用端到端向量量化变分自编码器，联合执行AF分类和ECG重建。包含两个核心组件：(1) 局部-全局对比模块，学习噪声不变表征；(2) 自适应码本向量量化器，动态优化码本原型以对齐输入数据分布，防止码本崩塌并增强泛化能力。

Result: 在标准12导联数据集内评测中达到最优性能；在七种跨数据集场景下均实现AUC>0.98；在基线漂移、电源线干扰和EMG伪影等真实噪声条件下仍保持高诊断准确率。

Conclusion: DCGCNet为鲁棒、可泛化且抗噪声的AF检测建立了新基准，展现出在真实临床环境中部署的潜力。

Abstract: \textbf{Background and Objective}: Reliable atrial fibrillation (AF) detection from electrocardiogram (ECG) signals remains challenging in real-world clinical settings due to variable lead configurations, cross-dataset domain shifts, and pervasive physiological and technical artifacts. So we develop a robust and generalizable deep learning model for accurate AF detection.\\ \textbf{Methods}: We propose the Dual-Codebook Graph Collaborative Network (DCGCNet), a novel end-to-end vector-quantized variational autoencoder that jointly performs AF classification and ECG reconstruction. DCGCNet introduces two key components: (1) a Local-Global Contrastive Module for learning noise-invariant representations, and (2) an Adaptive Codebook Vector Quantizer that dynamically refines codebook prototypes to better align with input data distributions, thereby preventing codebook collapse and enhancing generalization.\\ \textbf{Results}: DCGCNet achieves state-of-the-art performance in standard intra-dataset 12-lead evaluation and demonstrates exceptional cross-dataset generalization across seven diverse settings, consistently attaining AUC > 0.98 in all cases. Furthermore, it maintains high diagnostic accuracy under realistic noisy conditions, including baseline wander, powerline interference, and EMG artifacts.\\ \textbf{Conclusions}: DCGCNet establishes a new benchmark for robust, generalizable, and noise-resilient AF detection, showing strong potential for deployment in real-world clinical environments.

</details>


### [203] [ERASE: EaRly bAckpropagation SchEdule for Faster Training of Modern Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18469)
*Ergan Shang,Flavio Sales Truzzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ERASE方法，通过将Forward-Forward的detachment机制重新解释为调度原语，在轻量级代理模型的训练中实现前向和后向传播的流重叠执行，在大型CTR模型上提升最高9.51%的训练吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级代理模型的内核较小，难以充分利用现代加速器；传统训练将前向和后向传播作为不重叠的阶段调度，导致空闲计算能力无法被另一阶段的工作填补。

Method: 将Forward-Forward（FF）的detachment机制重新解释为一种调度原语：给定局部目标，detach块的输出可移除下游梯度依赖，使其后向传播在前向传播完成时即可就绪。ERASE在独立CUDA流上提前启动每个detached子图的后向传播，与后续前向工作重叠执行。

Result: 在轻量级transformer上验证了重叠执行及其局限（设备饱和的内核没有并发余地）；在大型点击率模型上，detach六个密集子架构使训练吞吐量最高提升9.51%，同时归一化熵接近基线。

Conclusion: ERASE通过流重叠有效利用了轻量级模型训练中的空闲计算能力，在不牺牲模型质量的前提下显著提升训练吞吐量。

Abstract: Lightweight proxy models enable rapid experimentation without repeatedly training frontier-scale systems, but their small kernels often leave modern accelerators underutilized. Conventional training compounds this inefficiency by scheduling the forward and backward passes as disjoint phases, so spare capacity in one cannot be filled by work from the other. We reinterpret the detachment mechanism of Forward-Forward (FF) as a scheduling primitive: given a local objective, detaching a block's output removes downstream gradient dependencies, making its backward pass ready when its forward pass finishes. ERASE launches each detached subgraph's backward pass early on a separate CUDA stream, overlapping it with subsequent forward work. Execution trace on a lightweight transformer demonstrates this overlap and its limit: a kernel that saturates the device leaves no capacity for concurrency. On a large-scale click-through-rate model, detaching six dense subarchitectures improves training throughput by up to $9.51\%$ while keeping normalized entropy close to the baseline.

</details>


### [204] [Physics-Unrolled Neural Operator for Wireless Field Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18495)
*Rafid Umayer Murshed,Saif Ur Rahman,Mingyue Tang,Elahe Soltanaghai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PU-HNO，一种三阶段级联的物理展开混合神经算子，用于从低保真射线跟踪输出和场景先验中预测高保真室内无线电地图，逐步捕获反射、衍射和散射效应，并在条件无偏标签噪声下证明能学习稳定的传播结构并超越自身训练标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线电地图对无线决策任务至关重要，但精细空间细节由复杂传播效应决定，高保真仿真成本高昂。机器学习虽能避免昂贵的逐场景高保真模拟，但大规模高质量训练标签难以生成：可负担的标签来自有限射线模拟，虽然比低保真输入丰富，但带有残余蒙特卡洛噪声。

Method: PU-HNO是一种三阶段级联架构，将反射、衍射和散射效应逐步建模，而非将无线电地图视为通用图像。该方法从低保真射线追踪输出和场景先验出发，通过物理展开的方式逐步捕获各传播机制；同时论文证明在条件无偏标签噪声下模型可学习稳定的传播结构。

Result: 在多种不同楼层平面图的实验中，PU-HNO在图像质量和无线部署指标上均优于图像到图像基线方法、无线学习模型以及单块式神经算子。

Conclusion: PU-HNO通过物理启发的级联设计有效解决了噪声训练标签下的高保真无线电地图预测问题，证明了物理驱动的神经算子架构在无线信道建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Radio maps are essential for wireless decision-making tasks such as access-point placement, coverage planning, and localization, but their fine spatial details are governed by complex propagation effects and are costly to simulate accurately. Machine learning offers a path to high-fidelity radio-map prediction without running expensive high-fidelity simulations for every scene. However, generating high-quality training labels at scale is also difficult: the affordable labels come from finite-ray simulations, which are richer than low-fidelity inputs but carry residual Monte Carlo noise. We address this challenge with Physics-Unrolled Hybrid Neural Operator (PU-HNO), a three-stage cascade that predicts high-fidelity indoor radio maps from low-fidelity ray-tracing outputs and scene priors by progressively capturing reflection, diffraction, and scattering effects, rather than treating radio maps as generic images. We prove that, under conditionally unbiased label noise, the model can learn stable propagation structure and outperform its own training labels. Experiments across diverse floorplans show that PU-HNO outperforms image-to-image baselines, wireless learning models, and monolithic neural operators across both image-quality and wireless deployment metrics.

</details>


### [205] [Tianmu-TC: Physics-constraints Generative Artificial Intelligence for Global Tropical Cyclone Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18500)
*Shiqi Zhang,Pan Mu,Cheng Huang,Hanting Yan,Yuchao Zhu,Jinglin Zhang,Shengyong Chen,Shoujuan Shu,Cong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Tianmu-TC，一个基于物理约束的生成式全球台风预测框架，在西北太平洋数据上训练，性能优于ECMWF等权威数值预报系统，计算成本显著更低，并在数据稀疏、异常路径、快速增强和减弱等挑战性场景中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 台风预测面临混沌大气和初始条件误差快速放大带来的不确定性挑战，传统数值天气预报和深度学习模型虽然取得进展，但计算成本高且在复杂气象场景下表现不佳。

Method: Tianmu-TC是一个基于物理约束的生成式AI框架，训练数据来自西北太平洋，通过物理约束机制生成可控输出，降低预测不确定性并提高可靠性。

Result: Tianmu-TC在全球海洋盆地的预测性能优于确定性和集合气象AI模型以及ECMWF等权威数值预报系统，计算成本显著更低，且在数据稀疏、异常路径、快速增强和减弱等挑战性场景中表现良好。

Conclusion: 物理约束的生成式AI为可靠、高效的全球台风预测提供了一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Tropical cyclones (TCs) pose severe risks from strong winds and heavy rainfall. However, forecasting their track and intensity remains challenging due to chaotic atmosphere and the rapid amplification of initial condition errors, leading to growing forecast uncertainty. While numerical weather prediction (NWP) and deep learning models have made progress, they remain computationally demanding and often fail under complex meteorological scenarios. Here, we present Tianmu-TC, a physics-constraints generative framework for global TC forecasting. Trained on Western North Pacific data, Tianmu-TC leverages physics-constraints to generate controllable outputs with reduced uncertainty thus improving forecast reliability. Experiments show Tianmu-TC outperforms deterministic and ensemble meteorological artificial intelligence models and authoritative NWP systems such as ECMWF in global ocean basins, with significantly lower computational cost. We further show Tianmu-TC performs well in challenging scenarios such as data sparsity, anomaly tracks, rapid intensification and weakening. These findings suggest physics-constraints generative AI offers a promising approach for reliable, efficient global TC forecasting.

</details>


### [206] [LLM-Powered Predictive Decision-Making for Sustainable Data Center Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18503)
*Hanzhao Wang,Jingxuan Wu,Yumeng Li,Yu Pan,Guanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的预测性调度系统，通过从源代码预测执行时间和能耗等关键指标，结合实时GPU调度算法来提升数据中心运营效率并降低环境影响。与数据中心合作实现了32%的能耗降低和30%的等待时间减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的工作负载（尤其是大语言模型）在数据中心中的快速增长引发了显著的能源和资源消耗问题，亟需有效的调度方案来减少环境影响。

Method: 该方法利用LLM从源代码预测执行时间和能耗等关键指标，预测模型后接实时调度算法来分配GPU资源，在优化能耗的同时兼顾排队延迟。系统设计注重快速推理、跨任务泛化能力和低训练数据需求。

Result: 与数据中心合作验证表明，系统实现了32%的能耗降低和30%的排队等待时间减少。

Conclusion: 该LLM驱动的预测性调度框架为数据中心可持续发展提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案，展示了在AI驱动基础设施中推进可持续目标的重要潜力。

Abstract: The growing demand for AI-driven workloads, particularly from Large Language Models (LLMs), has raised concerns about the significant energy and resource consumption in data centers. This work introduces a novel LLM-based predictive scheduling system designed to enhance operational efficiency while reducing the environmental impact of data centers. Our system utilizes an LLM to predict key metrics such as execution time and energy consumption from source code, and it has the potential to extend to other sustainability-focused metrics like water usage for cooling and carbon emissions, provided the data center can track such data. The predictive model is followed by a real-time scheduling algorithm that allocates GPU resources, aiming to improve sustainability by optimizing both energy consumption and queuing delays. With fast inference times, the ability to generalize across diverse task types, and minimal data requirements for training, our approach offers a practical solution for data center scheduling. This framework demonstrates strong potential for advancing sustainability objectives in AI-driven infrastructure. Through our collaboration with a data center, we achieved a 32% reduction in energy consumption and a 30% decrease in waiting time.

</details>


### [207] [Evaluating and Explaining Prompt Sensitivity of LLMs Using Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18539)
*Ruiyang Qin,Qingzhuo Wang,Tian Wang,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于交互（interactions）的细粒度分析方法来评估大语言模型的提示敏感性（prompt sensitivity），并提出了新的IPS指标，通过对50个开源LLMs的分析发现了四个降低提示敏感性的因素及其共同机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的提示敏感性评估仅通过比较LLM的最终输出来衡量，这种粗粒度指标无法解释提示敏感性产生的内部原因，需要更细粒度的分析工具来揭示其内在机制。

Method: 将LLM的输出分数分解为一组交互（interactions），每个交互代表一组输入变量之间的非线性关系；提出基于交互变化的Interaction-based Prompt Sensitivity (IPS)指标，通过量化引入细微提示变化时交互的变化来评估提示敏感性。
